## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-13T06:51:48.605Z

```
实现泰拉瑞亚 1.4.5.6 复刻仓库 game/（cwd）四件视觉/机制补齐。标杆=../Terarria1456/。素材已全量（TownNPCs/*_Shimmered/_Transformed、CageTop_0-4、Chain 族、GolemLights）。

1. **城镇 NPC 微光变体贴图**：src/entities/TownNPC.ts（你独占）已有 `shimmered` flag（入微光→标记+回家）。原版 TownNPCProfiles/Profiles.cs：altTexture==-1? shimmered 用 `TownNPCs/{Name}_Shimmered.png`（全 27 只变体表已拷）。实现：TownNPC 加只读 getter 返回"当前应使用的贴图 sheet id"——微光态换 TownNPCs 变体表；在 src/render/Renderer.ts 的 drawTownNPC 取 vnpc 处按该 getter 切表（映射表：vanillaId→TownNPCs 名，NPC_PORTRAITS 已有同构名字表可复用）。⚠Renderer.ts 有并行代理同时改：**Edit 前先 Read**，一处小改。
2. **动物学家狼人态 633**：_Transformed 表（BestiaryGirl_Default_Transformed.png 已拷）。原版 ShouldBestiaryGirlBeLycantrope（满月/血月等→变身）。TownNPC 633 在变身条件（查 NPC.cs ShouldBestiaryGirlBeLycantrope 行号）时切 Transformed 表（同 1 的切表机制）。变身条件接 moonPhase/血月/图鉴进度（图鉴 CompletionPercent≥某值? 查源码）。
3. **小动物笼放置渲染**：笼子 tile（TileID.Sets.Cage? tile 355-370 区，查 TEdit tiles.json 名 AnimalInCage/CritterCage）。原版 Main.cs DrawTile：笼=底座 tile 本体 + **CageTop_{0-4} 顶盖**（按笼型）+ 栖木+笼内小动物贴图（NPC 表缩放循环动画）。实现：在 tile 绘制路径（src/render/VanillaTiler.ts，你独占）识别笼族 tile（先 grep tiles.ts 找 cage 键），画 CageTop 顶盖（查 Main.cs DrawTile cage 段行号取顶盖偏移/缩放）+ 笼内 NPC 缩放动画（vnpc 现成）。若笼族 tile 尚未实装为可放置（createTile 有但放置链缺）则登记不做放置链，只做渲染侧。
4. **装饰链差集**：Chain_{n}（墙链 tile 221 区? 查 tiles：Chain 是 tile 80? no——grep tiles.json "chain"）原版悬挂链 tile 渲染。若仓库已有墙链 tile（grep src/data/tiles.ts chain）→ 只登记；若缺 → 按原版 Chain tile（TileID 221）渲染（竖直连续链环贴图 Chain.png 切片）。小成本即可，复杂则登记。
要求：行为引行号；测试 tests/town-variants.test.ts（切表 getter 纯函数 27 表全命中/狼人条件表/笼族 tile 识别）；vitest 绿+tsc 你的文件 0 错。汇报各件状态+登记项。

## 📎 Attachment · skill_listing · 2026-08-13T06:51:48.610Z

```
- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: "chart", "graph", "plot", "data viz", "visualization", "dashboard", "analytics", "visualize data", "categorical colors", "sequential / diverging palette", "stat tile", "sparkline", "heatmap", "legend", "axis", "tooltip", "chart colors", "color by series".
- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors ("from now on when X", "each time X", "whenever X", "before/after X") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.
- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: "rebind ctrl+s", "add a chord shortcut", "change the submit key", "customize keybindings".
- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.
- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).
SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).
- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).
- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation
- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review
- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch
```

262	    this.npcDoorUpdate(world, game);

264	    // ---- 原版 FindFrame 城镇分支（NPC.cs:70172-70262）----
265	    // 腾空=帧1；地面站定=帧0、计数清零；行走 frameCounter += |vx|*2+1，>6 推进，
266	    // 帧区间 2..frames-extra-1（越界回卷帧2）
267	    if (!this.onGround) {
268	      this.frame = 1;
269	      this.frameT = 0;
270	    } else if (Math.abs(this.vx) < 0.05) {
271	      this.frame = 0;
272	      this.frameT = 0;
273	    } else {
274	      const total = VANILLA_NPCS[String(this.vanillaId)]?.frames ?? 1;
275	      const extra = TOWN_NPC_IDS[this.npcKey]?.extra ?? 2;
276	      this.frameT += Math.abs(this.vx) * 2 + 1;
277	      if (this.frame < 2) this.frame = 2;
278	      if (this.frameT > 6) {
279	        this.frameT = 0;
280	        this.frame++;
281	      }
282	      if (this.frame >= total - extra) this.frame = 2;
283	    }
284	
285	  }

287	  /** 环境与互殴受击(NPC.cs:91534-91544 的 townNPC 分支三件套):
288	   *  ① 岩浆(Collision_LavaCollision :94511-94530):immun[255]=30 + StrikeNPC(50) 直伤
289	   *    (OnFire buff 24/420t 未接城镇 NPC buff 系统,略——登记)
290	   *  ② 溺水(NPC.CheckDrowning :96118-96154):水内每 7t breath--;breath≤0 后
291	   *    每 7t life-=2,归零一击 StrikeNPC(2);离水 breath+3/t cap 200
292	   *  ③ 敌怪互殴(GetHurtByOtherNPCs :93605-93690):任一 !friendly 且 damage>0 的
293	   *    NPC 接触 → StrikeNPC(DamageVar(attacker.damage), 6, 方向),immune[255]=30;
294	   *    dryadWard 荆棘反伤(num5/3)与 HurtingBees 未接,略
295	   *  Enemy.def.damage 已含难度缩放(ScaleStats),DamageVar≈1(Classic)直用。 */
296	  private envHurtUpdate(world: import('../world/World').World, game: GameHooks): void {
297	    if (this.dead) return;
298	    const st = world.store;
299	    if (this.lavaCd > 0) this.lavaCd--;
300	    if (this.npcVsCd > 0) this.npcVsCd--;
301	    const ci = st.idx(
302	      Math.max(0, Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor(this.cx / TILE))),
303	      Math.max(0, Math.min(st.h - 1, Math.floor(this.cy / TILE))),
304	    );
305	    // ① 岩浆(:94511-94530)
306	    if (st.liquid[ci] > 30 && st.liquidType[ci] === 2 && this.lavaCd <= 0) {
307	      this.lavaCd = 30;
308	      this.hurt(50, 0, 0, game);
309	    }
310	    // ② 溺水(:96118-96154;DrownCollision=头部浸水,取头顶格)
311	    const headY = Math.max(0, Math.floor((this.y + 6) / TILE));
312	    const hi = st.idx(Math.max(0, Math.min(st.w - 1, Math.floor(this.cx / TILE))), headY);
313	    const submerged = st.liquid[hi] > 30 && st.liquidType[hi] === 1;
314	    if (submerged) {
315	      if (++this.breathCounter >= 7) {
316	        this.breathCounter = 0;
317	        this.breath--;
318	        if (this.breath <= 0) {
319	          this.breath = 0;
320	          this.hp -= 2; // life-=2 直伤(不过防御,原版 :96137)
321	          if (this.hp <= 0) { this.hp = 1; this.hurt(2, 0, 0, game); } // :96141-96144 致死一击
322	          else game.addDamageNumber(this.cx, this.y, 2, false, '#FFD060');
323	        }
324	      }
325	    } else {
326	      this.breath = Math.min(200, this.breath + 3);
327	      this.breathCounter = 0;
328	    }
329	    // ③ 敌怪互殴(:93605-93690)
330	    if (this.npcVsCd <= 0) {
331	      for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
332	        const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean;
333	          damage?: number; def?: { damage?: number } };
334	        if (e.dead) continue;
335	        const dmg = e.damage ?? e.def?.damage ?? 0;
336	        if (dmg <= 0) continue;
337	        if (!(this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y)) continue;
338	        const dir = e.x + e.w / 2 > this.cx ? -1 : 1; // :93662 攻击者在右侧 → 向左击退
339	        this.npcVsCd = 30;                              // immune[255]=30
340	        this.hurt(dmg, dir * 6, 0, game);               // kb=6(:93661)
341	        break;
342	      }
343	    }
344	  }
345	
346	  /** 城镇 NPC 自卫攻击（AI_007 攻击态机,NPC.cs:54747-55538 + 触发门 :55621-55966）。
347	   *  返回 true = 攻击态中(调用方停走)。语义对照:
348	   *  - flag31 门 ≈ 空闲+站地+触发冷却尽+探测范围内有可打敌(flag16;stinky 分支无系统跳过)
349	   *  - 掷骰 rand(max(1,chance×num3))==0,num3=2(209 ÷3),:53813/:53562
350	   *  - 入态:ai[1]=AttackTime,面向目标;态内每 tick ai[1]--/localAI[3]++/vx×0.8
351	   *  - 发射档:localAI[3]==num46/55/65;连发链 `localAI[3]>档 → 推下一档`(:55066 族)
352	   *  - 退出:ai[1]<=0 → 冷却 localAI[1] = cdBase/2 + rand(cdRand)(:54927-54934 族)
353	   *  近似项:难度伤害缩放恒 Classic=1(Journey/Expert 切换未接);633 狼人变体/
354	   *  108 连发 1-3/160·663 落点弹重掷等特化在发射端按 note 近似 */
355	  private attackUpdate(world: import('../world/World').World, game: GameHooks): boolean {
356	    if (this.atkCd > 0) this.atkCd--;
357	    const atkType = TOWN_ATTACK_TYPE[this.vanillaId];
358	    if (atkType === undefined) return false;
359	    this.hardModeFlag = !!(world.flags as Record<string, boolean>).hardMode;
360	    if (this.atkState === 0) {
361	      // 触发门:站地 + 冷却尽 + 探测范围内有 LOS 敌(:55621 flag31 + :55864 族掷骰)
362	      if (!this.onGround || this.atkCd > 0 || this.talking) return false;
363	      const range = TOWN_DANGER_RANGE[this.vanillaId] ?? 200;
364	      const target = this.pickSideTarget(world, game, range);
365	      if (!target) return false;
366	      let chance = TOWN_ATTACK_CHANCE[this.vanillaId] ?? 1;
367	      let num3 = 2;                             // :53413(Skyblock ÷1.5 未接)
368	      if (this.vanillaId === 209) num3 /= 3;    // :53548 蒸汽朋克人三倍勤
369	      const gate = Math.max(1, Math.floor(chance * num3));
370	      if (Math.random() >= 1 / gate) return false;  // rand(gate)==0
371	      const params = this.atkParams(atkType);
372	      if (!params && atkType !== 3) return false;
373	      this.atkState = atkType === 0 ? 10 : atkType === 1 ? 12 : atkType === 2 ? 14 : 15;
374	      this.atkTimer = TOWN_ATTACK_TIME[this.vanillaId] ?? 30;
375	      this.atkTick = 0;
376	      this.atkBurstIdx = 0;
377	      this.atkNextShot = (params as TownShotParams | undefined)?.shootTick ?? 1;
378	      this.facing = (target as { cx: number }).cx < this.cx ? -1 : 1;
379	      return true;
380	    }
381	    // ---- 态内推进 ----
382	    this.atkTimer--;
383	    this.atkTick++;
384	    this.vx *= 0.8;
385	    const range = TOWN_DANGER_RANGE[this.vanillaId] ?? 200;
386	    if (this.atkState === 15) {
387	      this.swingUpdate(world, game);
388	    } else {
389	      const params = this.atkParams(TOWN_ATTACK_TYPE[this.vanillaId]) as TownShotParams;
390	      // 连发链:localAI[3] 超过当前档 → 推下一档(原版逐档 if 链)
391	      const burst = params.burst ?? [];
392	      while (this.atkBurstIdx < burst.length && this.atkTick > this.atkNextShot) {
393	        this.atkNextShot = burst[this.atkBurstIdx++];
394	      }
395	      if (this.atkTick === this.atkNextShot) this.shootAt(world, game, params, range);
396	    }
397	    if (this.atkTimer <= 0) {
398	      // 退出 + 冷却(:54927/:55199/:55397/:55529 族):localAI[1] = cdBase/2 + rand(cdRand)
399	      const p = this.atkParams(TOWN_ATTACK_TYPE[this.vanillaId]);
400	      const cdBase = (p as TownShotParams | undefined)?.cdBase ?? 10;
401	      const cdRand = (p as TownShotParams | undefined)?.cdRand ?? 1;
402	      this.atkState = 0;
403	      this.atkCd = Math.floor(cdBase / 2) + Math.floor(Math.random() * Math.max(1, cdRand));
404	    }
405	    return true;
406	  }
407	
408	  /** 当前类型的态参数(10/12/14;15 走 TOWN_SWING);hardMode 覆盖按 world.flags */
409	  private atkParams(atkType: 0 | 1 | 2 | 3): TownShotParams | undefined {
410	    const table = atkType === 0 ? TOWN_MELEE : atkType === 1 ? TOWN_BOW : atkType === 2 ? TOWN_MAGIC : undefined;
411	    if (!table) return undefined;
412	    const base = table[this.vanillaId];
413	    if (!base) return undefined;
414	    if (base.hardMode && this.hardModeFlag) return { ...base, ...base.hardMode } as TownShotParams;
415	    return base;
416	  }
417	
418	
419	  /** 挥击态(15):前方矩形近战判定(:55469-55494;原版逐 tick 弧形挥击,近似中段一击) */
420	  private swingUpdate(_world: import('../world/World').World, game: GameHooks): void {
421	    const sw = TOWN_SWING[this.vanillaId];
422	    if (!sw) return;
423	    const t = TOWN_ATTACK_TIME[this.vanillaId] ?? 15;
424	    if (this.atkTick !== Math.max(1, Math.floor(t / 2))) return; // 挥击中段
425	    const rect = sw.rect;
426	    const rx = this.facing === 1 ? this.cx : this.cx - rect;
427	    const ry = this.cy - rect / 2;
428	    let dmg = sw.dmg;
429	    let kb = sw.kb;
430	    if (this.vanillaId === 441 && this.givenName === 'Andrew') { dmg *= 2; kb *= 2; } // 猫单特典 :55431
431	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
432	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean;
433	        hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean };
434	      if (e.dead) continue;
435	      if (!(rx < e.x + e.w && rx + rect > e.x && ry < e.y + e.h && ry + rect > e.y)) continue;
436	      if (e.hurt(Math.round(dmg * townNpcDamageMult(0)), this.facing * kb, 0, game)) {
437	        game.addDamageNumber(e.x, e.y, dmg, false, '#FFD060');
438	      }
439	    }
440	  }
441	
442	  /** 探测范围内按侧取最近可打敌(:53887-53958 近侧目标;LOS=Collision.CanHit) */
443	  private pickSideTarget(world: import('../world/World').World, game: GameHooks, range: number):
444	    { cx: number; cy: number } | null {
445	    let bestL: { cx: number; cy: number; d: number } | null = null;
446	    let bestR: { cx: number; cy: number; d: number } | null = null;
447	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
448	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean;
449	        damage?: number; def?: { damage?: number } };
450	      if (e.dead) continue;
451	      // 原版扫描门(:53887):!friendly && damage>0 才算威胁(0 伤敌/友好不算)
452	      const edmg = e.damage ?? e.def?.damage ?? 0;
453	      if (edmg <= 0) continue;
454	      // 中心由 x/y/w/h 推导(敌实体有 cx/cy,但勿依赖——判距门曾因 NaN 恒假失效)
455	      const ecx = e.x + e.w / 2, ecy = e.y + e.h / 2;
456	      const dx = ecx - this.cx;
457	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, ecy - this.cy);
458	      if (d >= range) continue;
459	      if (!canHit(world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, e.x, e.y, e.w, e.h)) continue;
460	      const cand = { cx: ecx, cy: ecy, d };
461	      if (dx < 0) { if (!bestL || d < bestL.d) bestL = cand; }
462	      else { if (!bestR || d < bestR.d) bestR = cand; }
463	    }
464	    // num15 侧选择(:53960):两侧都有 → 距离近者;仅一侧 → 该侧
465	    if (bestL && bestR) return bestL.d <= bestR.d ? bestL : bestR;
466	    return bestL ?? bestR;
467	  }
468	
469	  /** 发射(:54903/:55175/:55325 三族瞄准公式统一在此) */
470	  private shootAt(world: import('../world/World').World, game: GameHooks, params: TownShotParams, range: number): void {
471	    const st = world.store;
472	    let proj = params.proj;
473	    let dmg = params.dmg;
474	    let speed = params.speed;
475	    let kb = params.kb;
476	    let spread = params.spread;
477	    // 229 PrettySafe 近距保命换炮弹(:55128-55135)
478	    const pretty = TOWN_PRETTY_SAFE[this.vanillaId];
479	    const target = this.pickSideTarget(world, game, range);
480	    if (params.panic && pretty && target && Math.hypot(target.cx - this.cx, target.cy - this.cy) < pretty
481	      && this.atkTick === params.shootTick) {
482	      proj = params.panic.proj; dmg = params.panic.dmg; speed = params.panic.speed;
483	      kb = params.panic.kb; spread = params.panic.spread;
484	    }
485	    // 209 三选一弹(:55139)
486	    if (this.vanillaId === 209 && !params.panic) {
487	      const pick = [134, 133, 135][Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)];
488	      proj = pick;
489	      const m: Record<number, [number, number, number, number, number]> = {
490	        134: [20, 13, 4, 0.1, 20], 133: [25, 10, 6, 0.2, 10], 135: [30, 12, 7, 0.2, 30],
491	      };
492	      [dmg, speed, kb, spread] = [m[pick][0], m[pick][1], m[pick][2], m[pick][3]];
493	    }
494	    // 瞄准:弓 = 目标上抬 aimLift 取向;近战/魔法 = aimLiftScaled×(dist/range 归一)上抬
495	    let vx = this.facing, vy = 0;
496	    if (target) {
497	      let lift = params.aimLift ?? 0;
498	      if (this.atkState === 10 || this.atkState === 14) {
499	        const dist = Math.hypot(target.cx - this.cx, target.cy - this.cy);
500	        lift = (params.aimLiftScaled ?? 0) * Math.max(0, Math.min(1, dist / range));
501	        vy = -1; // 近战/魔法默认上抛分量(初值 -UnitY)
502	      }
503	      const dx = target.cx - this.cx;
504	      const dy = target.cy - lift - this.cy;
505	      const dl = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
506	      vx = dx / dl; vy = dy / dl;
507	      if (Math.sign(vx) !== this.facing) { vx = this.facing; vy = this.atkState === 12 ? 0 : -1; }
508	    } else if (this.atkState !== 12) {
509	      vy = -1;
510	    }
511	    vx *= speed; vy *= speed;
512	    // 散布(Utils.RandomVector2 ±spread;近战态恒 0)
513	    if (spread > 0) {
514	      vx += (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * spread;
515	      vy += (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * spread;
516	    }
517	    const dmgFinal = Math.round(dmg * townNpcDamageMult(0));
518	    const sx = this.cx + this.facing * 16 - 5;
519	    const sy = this.cy - 2 - 5;
520	    // 落点弹(160/663):目标区域生成 velocity=0;实心重掷近似 ≤5 次
521	    if (speed === 0 && target) {
522	      for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) {
523	        const px = target.cx - 16 + Math.random() * 32;
524	        const py = target.cy - 16 + Math.random() * 32;
525	        if (!st.isSolid(Math.floor(px / TILE), Math.floor(py / TILE))) {
526	          game.spawnTownShot?.(new TownShot(px - 5, py - 5, 0, 0, dmgFinal, kb, proj, { noGravity: true }));
527	          return;
528	        }
529	      }
530	      return;
531	    }
532	    game.spawnTownShot?.(new TownShot(sx, sy, vx, vy, dmgFinal, kb, proj, {
533	      life: this.vanillaId === 588 ? 480 : undefined,
534	    }));
535	  }
536	
537	  /** 城镇 NPC 开关门（原版 NPC.cs:54478-54500/54243-54252，含高门 389 族 ShiftTallGate） */
538	  private npcDoorUpdate(world: import('../world/World').World, game: GameHooks) {
539	    const st = world.store;
540	    const DOOR_OPEN = TILE_BY_KEY_DOOR_TILES['door_open'] ?? -1;
541	    const GATE_OPEN = TILE_BY_KEY_DOOR_TILES['tall_gate_open'] ?? -1;
542	    // 过门关门：中心距门 >2 格 → 关；>4 格或换层 → 放弃（原版 54243-54252）
543	    if (this.closeDoor) {
544	      const tx = Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(this.cy / TILE);
545	      if (Math.abs(tx - this.doorX) > 2 || Math.abs(ty - this.doorY) > 4) {
546	        const doorType = st.type[st.idx(this.doorX, this.doorY)];
547	        if (Math.abs(tx - this.doorX) <= 4 && Math.abs(ty - this.doorY) <= 4) {
548	          if (doorType === DOOR_OPEN) closeDoorTile(st, this.doorX, this.doorY);
549	          else if (doorType === GATE_OPEN) {
550	            // 高门关门（原版 54266-54274：ShiftTallGate(closing:true)，tile 389 → 388）
551	            shiftTallGate(st, this.doorX, this.doorY, true, () => game.playSfx('door_close', 0.8, this.cx, this.cy));
552	          }
553	        }
554	        this.closeDoor = false;
555	      }
556	      return;
557	    }
558	    // 撞门开门：前方碰撞点是关着的普通门（10）或高门（388）
559	    // （原版 54478-54500：Next(10)==0；普通门两方向回退，高门 ShiftTallGate(closing:false)）
560	    if (!this.hitWall) return;
561	    const fx = Math.floor((this.cx + this.facing * (this.w / 2 + 2)) / TILE);
562	    const fy = Math.floor((this.y + this.h - 8) / TILE);
563	    for (const dy of [0, -1]) {
564	      const x = fx, y = fy + dy;
565	      if (x < 1 || y < 1 || x >= st.w - 1 || y >= st.h - 1) continue;
566	      const t = st.type[st.idx(x, y)];
567	      if (Math.random() >= 0.1) return;   // 原版 Next(10)==0
568	      if (t === TILE_BY_KEY_DOOR_TILES['door_closed']) {
569	        const before = st.type[st.idx(x, y)];
570	        const opened = openDoor(st, x, y, this.facing) || openDoor(st, x, y, -this.facing);
571	        if (opened && before !== st.type[st.idx(x, y)]) {
572	          this.closeDoor = true;
573	          this.doorX = x; this.doorY = y;
574	          game.playSfx('door_open', 0.8, this.cx, this.cy);
575	        }
576	        return;
577	      }
578	      if (t === TILE_BY_KEY_DOOR_TILES['tall_gate_closed']) {
579	        const anchor = shiftTallGate(st, x, y, false, () => game.playSfx('door_open', 0.8, this.cx, this.cy));
580	        if (anchor) {
581	          this.closeDoor = true;
582	          this.doorX = anchor[0]; this.doorY = anchor[1];
583	        }
584	        return;
585	      }
586	    }
587	  }

590	}

220	
221	  /** 原版图块表取帧（col,row 从 0 起）。越界/缺失返回 null */
222	  vframe(sheetId: number, col: number, row: number): DrawRect | null {
223	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
224	    if (!m) return null;
225	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);  // 懒加载兜底(正常路径 preloadVanillaWorld 已就绪)
226	    if (!img) return null;
227	    if (col < 0 || row < 0 || col >= m.cols || row >= m.rows) return null;
228	    return { img, sx: col * m.stride[0], sy: row * m.stride[1], sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
229	  }
230	
231	  /** 原版表内任意像素偏移取帧（style/显式帧：18px 步长的 frameX/frameY 直用） */
232	  vframeAt(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number): DrawRect | null {
233	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
234	    if (!m) return null;
235	    // 与 vframe/vrect 同语义懒加载（ensureVImage）：place_v_* 物品图标走本方法,
236	    // 此前直接 vimages.get——表未载时不发加载请求,宝箱内家具类物品图标永久回退
237	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
238	    if (!img) return null;
239	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + m.grid[0] > img.width || fy + m.grid[1] > img.height) return null;
240	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: m.grid[0], sh: m.grid[1] };
241	  }
242	
243	  /** 原版表内任意矩形（多格物体整体取图，如墓碑 2×2 = 34×34px） */
244	  vrect(sheetId: number, fx: number, fy: number, w: number, h: number): DrawRect | null {
245	    const m = this.vmeta(sheetId);
246	    if (!m) return null;
247	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.sheet);
248	    if (!img) return null;
249	    if (fx < 0 || fy < 0 || fx + w > img.width || fy + h > img.height) return null;
250	    return { img, sx: fx, sy: fy, sw: w, sh: h };
251	  }
252	
253	  /** 原版 NPC 贴图表取帧（纵向帧条，frameIdx 0-based）。
254	   *  未登记的 id 懒加载 vanilla/NPC_{id}.png（帧数来自 vanilla-npcs.json），首帧返回 null 下一帧生效 */
255	  private lazyNpcMeta = new Map<string, VanillaNpcMeta>();
256	  /** ⚠仅适用【纵向帧条】NPC 表。横向变体横条 NPC（如 594 风气球 = 8 列×32px 变体,
257	   *  Main.cs:23383 Frame(8,1,ai[2])）走此路径会把整条横排画出来——此类 NPC 必须
258	   *  在 Renderer.drawEnemy 加专属分支按列切片（见 drawWindyBalloon）。 */
259	  vnpc(npcId: number, frameIdx: number): DrawRect | null {
260	    let m: VanillaNpcMeta | undefined = this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)];
261	    if (!m) {
262	      const key = String(npcId);
263	      m = this.lazyNpcMeta.get(key);
264	      if (!m) {
265	        const sheet = `vanilla/NPC_${npcId}.png`;
266	        const img = this.vimages.get(sheet);
267	        if (!img) {
268	          const im = new Image();
269	          im.onload = () => this.vimages.set(sheet, im);
270	          im.src = `sprites/${sheet}`;
271	          return null;
272	        }
273	        const frames = (vanillaNpcFrames as Record<string, number>)[key] ?? 1;
274	        const fh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));
275	        const meta: VanillaNpcMeta = { sheet, frameW: img.width, frameH: fh, count: frames };
276	        this.lazyNpcMeta.set(key, meta);
277	        m = meta;
278	      }
279	    }
280	    const img = this.vimages.get(m.sheet);
281	    if (!img) return null;
282	    const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(m.count - 1, frameIdx));
283	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: idx * m.frameH, sw: m.frameW, sh: m.frameH };
284	  }
285	
286	  /** 原版 tile/item 英文名（全量表，未白名单的也有） */
287	  vTileName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.tileNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
288	  vItemName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.itemNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
289	
290	  vnpcMeta(npcId: number): VanillaNpcMeta | null {
291	    return this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)] ?? null;
292	  }

296	    const img = this.vimages.get(path);
297	    if (!img) return null;
298	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
299	  }
300	
301	  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/ 命名空间）。name 为白名单键，如 'UI_PanelBackground'。
302	   *  按需懒加载(消费方每帧重查,未就绪返回 null 自兜底) */
303	  vui(name: string): DrawRect | null {
304	    const path = this.uiFiles[name];
305	    if (!path) return null;
306	    const img = this.ensureUiImage(path);
307	    if (!img) return null;
308	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
309	  }

311	  private _uiPending = new Set<string>();
312	  private ensureUiImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
313	    const hit = this.uiimages.get(file);
314	    if (hit) return hit;
315	    if (this._uiPending.has(file)) return null;
316	    this._uiPending.add(file);
317	    const im = new Image();
318	    im.onload = () => { this.uiimages.set(file, im); this._uiPending.delete(file); };
319	    im.onerror = () => { this._uiPending.delete(file); };
320	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
321	    return null;
322	  }
323	
324	  /** 原版物品图标。构建期已 shelf-pack 进少数 Item_Atlas_k.png 图集(ix/iy/iw/ih
325	   *  子矩形,见 scripts/vanilla-atlas.mjs);旧单体条目(无矩形字段)回退整图语义。
326	   *  未加载时触发后台懒加载并返回 null(下帧生效) */
327	  vicon(itemId: number): DrawRect | null {
328	    const m = this.vanilla.items[String(itemId)];
329	    if (!m) return null;
330	    const img = this.ensureVImage(m.icon);
331	    if (!img) return null;
332	    if (m.ix !== undefined && m.iw !== undefined && m.ih !== undefined) {
333	      return { img, sx: m.ix, sy: m.iy ?? 0, sw: m.iw, sh: m.ih };
334	    }
335	    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
336	  }

338	  private _iconPending = new Set<string>();
339	  private _worldPreloaded = false;
340	  /** 懒加载完成回调(Game 注册 → ChunkCache 全量标脏:晚到的表重新烘焙
341	   *  已缓存的 chunk,否则 fallback 会永久烤进 canvas) */
342	  onVImageLoaded: ((file: string) => void) | null = null;
343	
344	  /** 预载文件清单(去重+decode)。tile/wall/NPC/misc 表与图标的统一底层 */
345	  async preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>, onProgress?: (p: number) => void): Promise<void> {
346	    const list = [...new Set(files)];
347	    let done = 0;
348	    await Promise.all(list.map((f) => new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
349	      if (this.vimages.has(f)) { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); return resolve(); }
350	      const img = new Image();
351	      img.onload = () => {
352	        this.vimages.set(f, img);
353	        // decode():预载阶段完成解码,否则 Chrome 延迟到首次 draw 才解码,
354	        // 大表(2000px 级)首帧仍会卡一拍——"预载"语义要求字节+解码双就绪
355	        img.decode().then(() => { done++; onProgress?.(done / list.length); resolve(); }, () => resolve());
356	        this.onVImageLoaded?.(f);
357	      };
358	      img.onerror = () => resolve();
359	      img.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(f)}`;
360	    })));
361	  }
362	
363	  /** 按图块 sheet id + 墙 id 预载对应贴图表。
364	   *  Game 用出生点区域类型扫描调用——只载画面涉及的表(出生点半径内实测仅
365	   *  22/378 张图块表),而不是全量 ~750 张(~250MB 解码) */
366	  preloadTileSheetsFor(tileSheets: Iterable<number>, wallIds: Iterable<number>): Promise<void> {
367	    const files = new Set<string>();
368	    for (const id of tileSheets) {
369	      const m = this.vanilla.tiles[String(id)];
370	      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
371	    }
372	    for (const id of wallIds) {
373	      const m = this.vanilla.walls[String(id)];
374	      if (m) files.add(m.sheet);
375	    }
376	    return this.preloadFiles(files);
377	  }
378	
379	  /** 预载常驻杂项(树冠/液体/瀑布/电路)+ NPC 表(小动物)——出生点必有,量小全载 */

So the vanilla logic:
- `UpdateAltTexture` (NPC.cs:91254):
  - altTexture = 0 default; party flag (excluding 441/453/633) → 1; type==633 && ShouldBestiaryGirlBeLycantrope() → 2.
- `ShouldBestiaryGirlBeLycantrope()` (NPC.cs:94961): blood moon && !dayTime → true; else if moonPhase == 0 → !dayTime; else false. I.e.:
  - If bloodMoon: return !dayTime (i.e. blood moon night)
  - If moonPhase == 0 (full moon): return !dayTime
  - Else false.

```csharp
if (!Main.bloodMoon || Main.dayTime)
{
    if (Main.moonPhase == 0)
    {
        return !Main.dayTime;
    }
    return false;
}
return true;
```
So if NOT bloodMoon OR dayTime (i.e., not a blood moon night): check moonPhase==0 → return !dayTime (full moon night). Else false.
If blood moon AND night: return true.

So lycanthrope = (bloodMoon && !dayTime) || (moonPhase == 0 && !dayTime).

				{

				}
			}

		}

<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Now I understand the full vanilla model:
- Town NPC texture = `Images/TownNPCs/{Name}` profiles. `townNpcVariationIndex` = 0 (normal) or 1 (shimmered) selects profile index in StackedNPCProfile.
- Within a profile: altTexture 1 → `_Party`, altTexture 2 → `_Transformed` (BestiaryGirl), altTexture 3 → `_Credits`.
- So full path: `Images/TownNPCs/{Name}_Default{,_Party,_Transformed,_Credits}.png` or `Images/TownNPCs/Shimmered/{Name}_Default{,...}.png`.

Where is townNpcVariationIndex set? In NPC.GetShimmered / CheckShimmer. Let me check. Also `isShimmerVariant`. Let me grep.
```

// 增量补齐（2026-08-11）：473-476/590/591/594/628/629/631/634/635/692 十三条取自

// Terarria1456/NPC.cs SetDefaults :17338-17367（aiStyle 9，字段对齐 30/112 同族；贴图
// NPC_665/666.png 已在 sprites，此前纯数据缺口）。
// 星璇塔增量（2026-08-11）：403/404（星尘蠕虫身/尾，字段复制 402 改宽高——1456 无独立
// SetDefaults，原版由 DrawNPC case 402 自绘 trail；本仓蠕虫链 spawnWormChain 需要
// 头+1/头+2 条目）、408（小水流入侵者，复制 407 缩小——1456 无独立 SetDefaults）。
// aiStyle 与 AI_XXX 方法的对应见 NPC.cs AI() 分发；此处先覆盖高频家族，
// 未移植 AI 家族的 aiStyle 回退到最接近的已移植行为（注释标注）。
import vanillaNpcsJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla-npcs.json';
import vanillaDataJson from '../../public/sprites/vanilla.json';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from './items';
import { TILE_DEFS } from './tiles';

/** tile id → key 反查（biomeAt 群系判定用） */
const TILE_KEY_NAME: string[] = TILE_DEFS.map((d) => d.key);

// （旧掉落表已退役：掉落走 src/drops/NpcDrops.ts 规则树——
//  tools/extract-npcdrops.mjs 结构化提取 ItemDropDatabase.cs，含专家/大师/条件/链语义）

/** 原版物品 id → 本仓库 item key（vanilla.json 的 key 是 PascalCase，ITEM_BY_KEY 多为 snake_case；
 *  未注册的返回 null 跳过） */
export const vanillaItemKey = (() => {
  const map = new Map<number, string | null>();
  const items = (vanillaDataJson as unknown as { items: Record<string, { key?: string }> }).items ?? {};
  return (itemId: number): string | null => {
    if (map.has(itemId)) return map.get(itemId)!;
    const meta = items[String(itemId)];
    let key: string | null = null;
    if (meta?.key) {
      const snake = meta.key.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1_$2').toLowerCase();
      if (ITEM_BY_KEY[snake] != null) key = snake;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[meta.key] != null) key = meta.key;
      // vi_NNN 导入物品（items.ts 的全量原版物品命名）：vi_<id> 或 vi_<id>_<snake>
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}`;
      else if (ITEM_BY_KEY[`vi_${itemId}_${snake}`] != null) key = `vi_${itemId}_${snake}`;
    }
    map.set(itemId, key);
    return key;
  };
})();

export interface VanillaNpc {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  frames: number;
  lifeMax: number;
  damage: number;
  defense: number;
  knockBackResist: number;
  aiStyle: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
  npcSlots: number;
  noGravity: boolean;
  noTileCollide: boolean;
  friendly: boolean;
  townNPC: boolean;
  HitSound: string;   // SoundID 名（NPCHitN / NPCDeathN）
  DeathSound: string;
  scale: number;
  /** SetDefaults alpha：每类型静态不透明度基线（渲染 1-alpha/255，NPC.Opacity；无通用渐隐） */
  alpha?: number;
  /** SetDefaults behindTiles：绘制在 tile 层之下（钻地族被前景方块遮挡，
   *  Main.cs:56457 DrawNPCs(behindTiles:true) 在实心 tile 之前绘制）。
   *  全表 46 id 取自 1456 SetDefaults（7-15/39-41/69/72/95-101/113-115/117-119/
   *  134-136/375/398/510-515/542-545/549/621-623）——飞龙 87/88 同为 aiStyle6 但
   *  非钻地不在表内 */
  behindTiles?: boolean;
  /** SetDefaults color：非 default 时 Main.DrawNPC 二次绘制同贴图（GetColor 逐像素乘法贴轮廓） */
  color?: number[];  // [r, g, b, a]
  critter?: boolean;  // NPCID.Sets.CountsAsCritter 小动物（tools/extract-critters.mjs 提取）

- lidStyle >= 3: 3x2 cages (2-tall) → 36px.
- For the top tile: draw the base texture shifted down 8px with top 8px cut (so the cage body starts 8px lower), then draw CageTop texture at position -2px Y with rect (frameX (mod 108 for style 0), 0, tileWidth, 10).

	}

	private int GetWaterAnimalCageFrame(int x, int y, int tileFrameX, int tileFrameY)
	{
		int num = x - tileFrameX / 18;
		int num2 = y - tileFrameY / 18;
		return num / 2 * (num2 / 3) % Main.cageFrames;
	}

	private int GetSmallAnimalCageFrame(int x, int y, int tileFrameX, int tileFrameY)
	{
		int num = x - tileFrameX / 18;
		int num2 = y - tileFrameY / 18;
		return num / 3 * (num2 / 3) % Main.cageFrames;
	}

	private int GetBigAnimalCageFrame(int x, int y, int tileFrameX, int tileFrameY)
	{
		int num = x - tileFrameX / 18;
		int num2 = y - tileFrameY / 18;
		return num / 6 * (num2 / 4) % Main.cageFrames;
	}

	public static void GetScreenDrawArea(bool useOffscreenRange, out Vector2 drawOffSet, out int firstTileX, out int lastTileX, out int firstTileY, out int lastTileY)
	{
		Vector2 scaledPosition = Main.Camera.ScaledPosition;
		Vector2 scaledSize = Main.Camera.ScaledSize;

For the top-left tile of the object, x - frameX/18 = origin x, y - frameY/18 = origin y. So slot = originX/6 * originY/4 % 8.

- AnimateTiles_CritterCages advances state machines per cage family per slot — with randomized delays and different frame loops per family (bunny: hop sequences 0/1, sniff 2-3, ear-twitch 4-10...; these are family-specific state machines).

This is a LOT of per-family animation state machines (bunnyCageFrame, squirrelCageFrame, mallardCageFrame, duckCageFrame, grebeCageFrame, seagullCageFrame, birdCageFrame, blueBirdCageFrame, redBirdCageFrame, macawCageFrame, scorpionCageFrame, penguinCageFrame, seahorseCageFrame, pufferfishCageFrame, snailCageFrame, snail2CageFrame, frogCageFrame, grasshopperCageFrame, mouseCageFrame, wormCageFrame, slugCageFrame, maggotCageFrame, ratCageFrame, ladybugCageFrame, waterStriderCageFrame, turtleCageFrame, owlCageFrame, fishBowlFrame, lavaFishBowlFrame, fairyJarFrame).

 *  (AllowsWind 墙集合未提取,任何墙挡风为保守近似) */
function inAPlaceWithWind(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, w2: number, h2: number): boolean {
  if (y >= renderEnv.worldSurface) return false;
  for (let i = 0; i < w2; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < h2; j++) {
      if (!st.inBounds(x + i, y + j)) continue;
      const ti = st.idx(x + i, y + j);
      if (st.liquid[ti] > 0 || st.wall[ti] > 0) return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

  const HALLOW = new Set(['v_117_pearlstone_block', 'v_109_hallowed_grass_block', 'v_116_pearlsand_block']);
  if (st.inBounds(x, y + 1)) {
    const k = TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(x, y + 1)]]?.key ?? '';
    if (HALLOW.has(k)) return 2;
    if (CRIMSON.has(k)) return 3;
    if (CORRUPT.has(k)) return 4;
  }
  const beach = 380; // WorldGen.beachDistance 近似
  return x < beach || x > w - beach ? 1 : 0;
}

// 城镇 NPC 受击链回归(原版三源):
// ① 玩家弹幕/近战 = 巫毒窄门(Damage_PVE_Inner :11970-11972:仅向导 22+向导娃 267 /
//    裁缝师 54+裁缝娃 1307);② 敌怪弹/陷阱弹 = hostile 分支恒命中(:11975 flag2);
// ③ 敌怪接触(GetHurtByOtherNPCs :93605)/岩浆(:94511)/溺水(:96118)走 TownNPC 内置。
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { TownNPC } from '../src/entities/TownNPC';
import { Inventory } from '../src/items/Inventory';
import { hitTownNpcs } from '../src/entities/projTargets';
import { TILE } from '../src/core/constants';

function makeEnv(npcList: TownNPC[], enemyList: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }> = [], killGuide = false) {
  const world = new World(200, 200, 100, 'hurt-test');
  for (let x = 20; x <= 180; x++) world.store.setTileSilent(x, 100, 1);
  const game = {
    world,
    player: { x: -9999, y: -9999, w: 0, h: 0, dead: true, equipStats: { killGuide, killClothier: false } },
    enemies: () => enemyList,
    npcs: () => npcList,
    spawnParticles: () => {}, playSfx: () => {}, playSfxFiles: () => {},
    addDamageNumber: () => {}, critters: () => [],
  } as never;
  return { world, game };
}

const box = { x: 100 * 16 - 10, y: 98 * 16, w: 20, h: 20 };

describe('城镇 NPC 受击链(巫毒门/hostile/环境)', () => {
  it('玩家弹幕无娃娃:向导/裁缝师均免疫(原版 :11970-11972 窄门)', () => {
    const guide = new TownNPC('guide', 100 * 16, 99 * 16, new Inventory());
    const { game } = makeEnv([guide], [], false);
    const hp0 = guide.hp;
    expect(hitTownNpcs(box, game, 30, 2, 'playerProj')).toBe(false);
    expect(guide.hp).toBe(hp0);
  });

  it('玩家弹幕 + 向导巫毒娃娃:向导(22)可伤,其他 NPC 仍免疫', () => {
    const guide = new TownNPC('guide', 100 * 16, 99 * 16, new Inventory());
    const merch = new TownNPC('merchant', 102 * 16, 99 * 16, new Inventory());
    const { game } = makeEnv([guide, merch], [], true);
    const g0 = guide.hp, m0 = merch.hp;
    hitTownNpcs(box, game, 30, 2, 'playerProj');
    expect(guide.hp).toBeLessThan(g0);      // 22 + killGuide → 命中
    expect(merch.hp).toBe(m0);              // 17 无门 → 免疫
  });

  it('敌怪弹(hostile 分支):任意城镇 NPC 恒命中(原版 :11975 flag2)', () => {
    const merch = new TownNPC('merchant', 100 * 16, 99 * 16, new Inventory());
    const { game } = makeEnv([merch], [], false);
    const m0 = merch.hp;
    expect(hitTownNpcs(box, game, 25, 0, 'hostile')).toBe(true);
    expect(merch.hp).toBeLessThan(m0);
  });

  it('敌怪接触(GetHurtByOtherNPCs :93605):重叠敌伤 NPC,30t 专属冷却', () => {
    const guide = new TownNPC('guide', 100 * 16, 99 * 16, new Inventory());
    guide.stationary = true;
    const enemy = { x: 100 * 16 + 4, y: 96 * 16, w: 20, h: 40, dead: false, damage: 40 };
    const { game } = makeEnv([guide], [enemy]);
    guide.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game); // 首帧即应互殴

// 世界门面：TileStore + 元数据 + 实体容器 + 昼夜时钟
import { TileStore } from './TileStore';
import { WeatherState, applyWeatherSave, weatherToSave, type WeatherSave } from './weather/Weather';
import { T, TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';
import { VI } from '../data/itemKeys';
import { newMoonEventState } from './MoonEvent';
import { newOldOnesArmyState } from './OldOnesArmy';
import { newLunarEventState } from './LunarEvent';
import type { FurnitureItemRecord } from './FurnitureItems';
import { BestiaryTracker, type BestiarySave } from '../data/Bestiary';

export interface ChestData { x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number; prefix?: number } | null>; locked?: boolean; }

/** 死亡掉钱保留比例（返回值 = 留在身上的份额；Player.DropCoins :53351-53390）：
 *  经典/旅程世界掉一半 / 专家掉 3/4 / 大师全掉——按**世界难度** Main.GameMode
 *  （0 经典 1 专家 2 大师 3 旅程按经典，Main.cs:2775-2780）。角色难度只决定
 *  是否走 DropCoins（见 Appearance.dropsCoinsOnDeath），不决定档位 */
export function deathCoinKeepFraction(worldDifficulty: number): number {
  // 原版 Player.DropCoins :53351-53380：num2 = stack/2(经典)/stack/4(专家)/0(大师) 是
  // **留在身上的份额**（num3 = stack - num2 才落地）——曾把 num2 误当掉出份额写反。
  if (worldDifficulty === 2) return 0;      // masterMode：num2=0 全掉（:53359）
  if (worldDifficulty === 1) return 0.25;   // expertMode：num2=stack/4 → 掉 3/4（:53356）
  return 0.5;                              // 经典/旅程：num2=stack/2 → 掉 1/2（:53353）
}

export class Clock {
  /** 昼夜边界（1:1 Main.time：昼 4:30AM-7:30PM=54000t、夜 32400t；0=午夜线性 24h） */
  static readonly DAWN = 4.5 / 24;   // 0.1875
  static readonly DUSK = 19.5 / 24;  // 0.8125
  timeOfDay: number = 8.25 / 24;   // 0-1，0=午夜 0.5=正午（起始 8:15AM = Main.time 13500）
  dayCount: number = 1;
  /** 月相 0-7（0=满月 4=新月；每黎明 +1 mod 8，Main.cs:64877）。对话月相池/血月门用 */
  moonPhase: number = 0;
  /** 血月旗标（dusk roll / dawn 清除，Main.cs:64817-64831） */
  bloodMoon: boolean = false;
  /** 日食旗标（Main.eclipse，随存档持久化——WorldFile.cs:1277-1278 写 / 2070-2072
   *  读，serialize meta.eclipse 同语义）：黎明 roll（Main.cs:64899-64920，
   *  hardMode && downedMechBossAny && 1/20）/ 日耀碑牌召唤（Player.cs:43308，白天），
   *  夜幕清除（Main.cs:64721-64725）——全天持续，只影响白天刷怪段。
   *  触发/清除实现在 world/Eclipse.ts */
  eclipse: boolean = false;
  /** 圣诞季（现实日期 12/15-31，checkXMas Main.cs:13054） */
  xMas: boolean = false;
  /** 万圣季（现实日期 10/20-11/10，checkHalloween Main.cs:13093） */
  halloween: boolean = false;
  readonly dayLengthMs: number;
  constructor(dayLengthMs: number) { this.dayLengthMs = dayLengthMs; }
  tick(dtMs: number) {
    // 原版 Main.time 恒速 tick（1t/帧）：dayLengthMs=全周期 24min 时，0.625/0.375 的
    // 昼夜窗口自然得出 15min/9min——★勿再按段调速（曾除反导致昼夜缩水，探针抓回）
    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;
    while (this.timeOfDay >= 1) { this.timeOfDay -= 1; this.dayCount++; }
  }
  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > Clock.DAWN && this.timeOfDay < Clock.DUSK; }
  get dayFactor(): number {
    // 昼夜平滑系数：白天 1，夜晚 0.32（月光下地表仍可见，火把不再像贴在黑幕上），晨昏过渡
    const t = this.timeOfDay;
    if (t > Clock.DAWN + 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK - 0.04) return 1;
    if (t >= Clock.DUSK - 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK + 0.04) return 1 - (t - (Clock.DUSK - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
    if (t >= Clock.DUSK + 0.04 || t < Clock.DAWN - 0.04) return 0.32;
    return 0.32 + (t - (Clock.DAWN - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
  }
  get hourFloat(): number { return this.timeOfDay * 24; }
}

export class World {
  store!: TileStore;   // definite assignment(读档 skipStore 路径由 fromPacket 注入)
  /** 本帧 SceneMetrics 快照（Game 每帧写；Enemy 判 zoneGraveyard 等只读用） */
  scene: import('./SceneMetrics').SceneFlags | null = null;
  /** 世界难度 = 原版 Main.GameMode（Main.cs:2677，ActiveWorldFileData.GameMode）：
   *  0 经典 / 1 专家 / 2 大师 / 3 旅程。与角色难度（Appearance.difficulty 0软/1中/2硬/3旅）
   *  是**两条独立轴**——世界难度管掉落倍率/吸血预算/掉钱档/旅行商店等 gameplay 门；
   *  角色难度只管死亡惩罚（软核掉钱/中核掉物品/硬核角色消亡）。创建 UI 写入、
   *  存档 header 持久化、wld 导入按 WldParser.gameMode 回填（旧档/worker 包缺省=0 经典） */
  difficulty = 0;
  /** 流星雨剩余计数(WorldGen.meteorShowerCount,WorldFile.cs:1069/:177 持久化)。
   *  消耗:UpdateWorld :71771-71810 每 tick 1/4 掷一颗 1078 伤害碎块,计数-1;
   *  晷快进直接清零(:71774-71777) */
  meteorShowerCount = 0;
  // ---- 夜间小动物概率五件套（NPC.setFireFlyChance，NPC.cs:94557-94610）----

But wait — world.scene is only set for the player's scene scan? `world.scene: SceneFlags | null` — "Game writes per frame". Town NPCs far from player may share the global scene. Renderer.drawTownNPC uses `this.scene?.partyUp` (Renderer.scene) for party. If I compute altTexture from world.scene in fixedUpdate, and Renderer uses its own scene for party — there might be mismatch. Hmm.

Cleaner: make the getter purely derived from a "context" the Renderer already has. But the task explicitly says: "TownNPC add a read-only getter returning 'the texture sheet id that should currently be used'". So the getter should be on TownNPC and read-only (no setter).

Approach: keep a module-level "current world context" that is updated by... hmm, that's stateful and ugly.

Alternative approach that fits both: the getter takes no args but computes from fields that fixedUpdate refreshes:
- `this.shimmered` (already a field)
- `this.altTexture` (new field, updated each tick in fixedUpdate from world.clock + world.scene?.partyUp)

That's not a "pure" getter, but it's a read-only getter over state. And the test "switch-table getter pure function hits all 27 tables" — the test can call the pure function `townNpcProfileSheet(vanillaId, shimmered, altTexture)` directly for all ids (that's the "pure function"). Good: I'll expose BOTH:
- pure `townNpcProfileSheet(id, shimmered, alt)` in data module (testable, all 27+ tables hit)
- `TownNPC.townSheet` getter = `townNpcProfileSheet(this.vanillaId, this.shimmered, this.altTexture)`

And `altTexture` as a public readonly-ish field computed per tick (documented as mirroring UpdateAltTexture). Since Renderer needs it too, and party img path already exists in Renderer, I'll restructure the Renderer site minimally:

```ts
// Profile sheet switching (Main.cs:22304-22309 DrawNPCCheckAlt → GetTextureNPCShouldUse):
// Microshimmer state switches to the TownNPCs variant table (Shimmered/{Name}_Default...),
// party/werewolf alt switches to _Party/_Transformed variant of the same table

let r: DrawRect | null;
if (sheetImg) {
  const count = this.atlas.vnpcMeta(n.vanillaId)?.count ?? 1;
  const fh = Math.floor(sheetImg.height / count);
  const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(count - 1, n.frame));
  r = { img: sheetImg, sx: 0, sy: idx * fh, sw: sheetImg.width, sh: fh };
} else {
  r = n.bound ? this.boundNpcSheet(n) : this.atlas.vnpc(n.vanillaId, n.frame);
}

  106: 54, 219: 54, 220: 54, 228: 54, 231: 54, 243: 54, 247: 54,

/** 回卷上限显式覆盖（默认 floor(素材高/pitch)；素材含未用行时以原版回卷为准） */
const TILE_ANIM_FRAMES: Record<number, number> = {

/** 篝火族（TileDrawing.cs:6124-6133 Campfires 覆盖）：pitch=36（非默认 38！素材帧块
 *  36px 无缝排布——曾按默认 38 导致每帧 +2px 漂移，火焰像胶片滚动）；
 *  frameY≥36 的行（熄灭/灰烬变体）恒取静止帧 252 */
export function campfireYOffset(frameY: number): number {
  return frameY < 36 ? animFrameIdx(215, 324) * 36 : 252;
}

/** 全局动画时钟（Game 主循环每帧 +1；暂停时冻结） */
export const tileAnim = { tick: 0 };

/** animFrameIdx/animYOffset 共用的 pitch 取值（无动画表项返回 0 表示不适用） */
export function tileAnimPitch(sheet: number): number {
  return TILE_ANIM_PITCH[sheet] ?? 38;
}

/** 该 sheet 当前动画帧索引（无动画表项/单帧返回 0）。
 *  sheetHeightPx = 素材总像素高（rows×18）；帧数 = TILE_ANIM_FRAMES 覆盖
 *  或 floor(sheetHeightPx / pitch) */
export function animFrameIdx(sheet: number, sheetHeightPx: number): number {
  const rate = TILE_ANIM_RATE[sheet];
  if (!rate) return 0;
  const frames = TILE_ANIM_FRAMES[sheet] ?? Math.floor(sheetHeightPx / tileAnimPitch(sheet));
  if (frames <= 1) return 0;
  return Math.floor(tileAnim.tick / rate) % frames;
}

/** 绘制时的 frameY 偏移（= 帧索引 × pitch） */
export function animYOffset(sheet: number, sheetHeightPx: number): number {
  const rate = TILE_ANIM_RATE[sheet];
  if (!rate) return 0;
  return animFrameIdx(sheet, sheetHeightPx) * tileAnimPitch(sheet);
}

```

  }

  /** 每帧重绘脏 chunk:数量上限 maxN 之外再加时间预算 budgetMs——
   *  跑图/全量标脏时烘焙突发不再挤占帧预算(实测 87ms 尖峰来源) */
  flushDirty(maxN = 4, budgetMs = 6) {
    let n = 0;
    const t0 = performance.now();
    while (this.dirtyQueue.length && n < maxN) {
      const k = this.dirtyQueue.shift()!;
      this.dirtySet.delete(k);
      const cx = k & 0xffff, cy = (k >> 16) & 0xffff;
      if (this.chunks.get(k) !== undefined) continue; // 已重建
      this.get(cx, cy);
      n++;
      if (performance.now() - t0 > budgetMs) break; // 单 chunk 烘焙超预算也至少完成 1 个
    }
    this.lastFlushMs = performance.now() - t0;
    this.lastFlushCount = n;
  }

  /** 动画时钟推进（Game 每帧调用）：sheet/wallId 到达换帧行 tick → 只重建对应 chunk。
   *  原版语义 = AnimateTiles / DoUpdate_AnimateWalls 每 rate tick 推进一帧；
   *  帧内 chunk 复用零开销 */
  advanceAnim(): void {
    tileAnim.tick++;
    if (this.animChunksBySheet.size) {
      for (const [sheet, set] of this.animChunksBySheet) {
        const rate = TILE_ANIM_RATE[sheet];
        if (!rate || tileAnim.tick % rate !== 0) continue;
        for (const k of set) this.markDirty(k & 0xffff, (k >> 16) & 0xffff);
      }
    }
    if (this.animChunksByWall.size) {
      for (const [wallId, set] of this.animChunksByWall) {
        const rate = wallAnimRate(wallId);
        if (!rate || tileAnim.tick % rate !== 0) continue;
        for (const k of set) this.markDirty(k & 0xffff, (k >> 16) & 0xffff);
      }
    }
  }

  /** 取 chunk 双层画布（惰性生成，LRU 淘汰最久未用） */
  get(cx: number, cy: number): ChunkPair {
    const k = ChunkCache.key(cx, cy);
    let c = this.chunks.get(k);
    if (c) {
      // LRU:命中即刷新 recency(delete+set 移到 Map 尾部)。渲染循环每帧都 get
      // 可视 chunk,故屏上 chunk 永远最新、不会被误淘汰
      this.chunks.delete(k);
      this.chunks.set(k, c);
      return c;
    }
    c = this.renderChunk(cx, cy);
    this.chunks.set(k, c);
    while (this.chunks.size > ChunkCache.MAX_CHUNKS) {
      const oldest = this.chunks.keys().next().value as number | undefined;
      if (oldest === undefined) break;
      this.releasePair(this.chunks.get(oldest)); // 淘汰画布同样释放,防 detached 积压
      this.chunks.delete(oldest);
    }
    return c;
  }

  /** 树枝判定：TREE 且上下皆非 TREE、恰好一侧为 TREE（横向独连树干）。
   *  下方是实心地面的属于树根底座 —— 走规则表渲染底座贴图，不算枝干 */

  private neighborMask(x: number, y: number, type: number): number {
    const st = this.world.store;
    let mask = 0;
    const same = (nx: number, ny: number) => st.inBounds(nx, ny) && st.flags[st.idx(nx, ny)] && st.type[st.idx(nx, ny)] === type ? 1 : 0;
    mask |= same(x, y - 1);        // N
    mask |= same(x + 1, y) << 1;   // E
    mask |= same(x, y + 1) << 2;   // S
    mask |= same(x - 1, y) << 3;   // W
    mask |= same(x + 1, y - 1) << 4; // NE
    mask |= same(x + 1, y + 1) << 5; // SE
    mask |= same(x - 1, y + 1) << 6; // SW

export function cageFrameRow(familyIdx: number, slot: number, tick: number, frames: number): number {

  const idle = 30 + hash2(familyIdx * 31 + slot, 0) % 871;   // rand.Next(30,900)

So:
```ts
export function townNpcProfileSheet(id: number, shimmered: boolean, alt: number): string | null {
  const p = TOWN_NPC_PROFILE[id];
  if (!p) return null;
  const root = shimmered ? `vanilla/Shimmered_${p.name}` : `vanilla/${p.name}`;
  const uniqueParty = shimmered ? p.shimmerParty : p.party;
  if (alt === 1) return `${root}_Default${uniqueParty ? '_Party' : ''}.png`;
  if (alt === 2 && p.transform) return `${root}_Default_Transformed.png`;
  return `${root}_Default.png`;
}
```

Key: `num = TextureAssets.Npc[type].Height() / Main.npcFrameCount[type]` (NPC.cs:67301-67302). The frame step is computed from the CURRENT texture's height divided by npcFrameCount. So for the TaxCollector party sheet (1456 tall), num = 1456/25 = 58.24 → int 58. Hmm, that gives 58 vs base 56. Slight mismatch — vanilla quirk. Whatever vanilla does, the *count* stays npcFrameCount[type]=25.

So in my renderer, using `count = vnpcMeta(id).count` and `fh = floor(img.height / count)` exactly mirrors vanilla's `num = texture.Height / npcFrameCount`. For TaxCollector party: floor(1456/25) = 58 — the same (truncated) value vanilla uses. 

So my slicing `fh = Math.floor(img.height / count)` is exactly vanilla's formula. The existing party code already does this. My new code will too. No mismatch issue.

403	  ensureVImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
404	    const hit = this.vimages.get(file);
405	    if (hit) return hit;
406	    if (this._iconPending.has(file) || this._vImageFailed.has(file)) return null;
407	    this._iconPending.add(file);
408	    const im = new Image();
409	    im.onload = () => {
410	      this.vimages.set(file, im);
411	      this._iconPending.delete(file);
412	      this._vImageFailed.delete(file);
413	      this.onVImageLoaded?.(file);  // 晚到的表 → ChunkCache 重烘焙
414	    };
415	    im.onerror = () => {
416	      this._iconPending.delete(file);
417	      this._vImageFailed.add(file);   // 永久失败:不再重复请求,供消费方分级告警

419	    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;

[6, 4] : kind === 'small' ? h = Math.imul(h, 0xc2b2ae35); h ^= h >>> 16;\n  return h >>> 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * 笼内小动物动画帧行（≈ AnimateTiles_CritterCages Main.cs:13452-16530）。\n * 原版为逐族状态机（空闲 rand(30,900)t → 族专属动作段 5/10t 推进 → 回空闲，双空闲\n * 帧/循环重掷各族不同）；此处取同构确定性循环：空闲帧 0 停 [30,900)t（hash 种子，\n * 每 65536t 换一拍近似原版逐次重掷）→ 帧 1..frames-1 每 5t 一帧 → 回空闲。\n * @param family 动画族序号（CageFamily.idx）\n * @param slot   槽位（cageSlot）\n * @param tick   全局动画 tick（tileAnim.tick）\n * @param frames 该表帧行数（floor(表高/step)）\n */\nexport function cageFrameRow(family: number, slot: number, tick: number, frames: number): number {\n  if (frames <= 1) return 0;\n  const episode = Math.floor(tick / 65536);                       // 换拍周期\n  const idle = 30 + hash32(family * 131 + slot, episode) % 871;   // rand.Next(30, 900)\n  const action = (frames - 1) * ACTION_TICKS;\n  const cycle = idle + action;\n  const t = ((tick % cycle) + cycle) % cycle;\n  if (t < idle) return 0;\n  return 1 + Math.min(frames - 2, Math.floor((t - idle) / ACTION_TICKS));\n}\n\n/** 重烘焙节奏（ChunkCache.advanceAnim 用）：动作帧 5t/帧 → 每 5 tick 重烘焙含笼 chunk */\nexport function cageAnimRate(): number {\n  return ACTION_TICKS;\n}\n\nexport interface DrawSource { img: HTMLImageElement; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number }\n\n/**\n * 笼族一格绘制（TileDrawing.cs:1670-1716 顶盖段 1:1）。\n * @param r       drawVanillaCell 已取的本格帧（vframeAt 结果，含 18px 步长源坐标）\n * @param sheetH  笼表总像素高（算帧行数）\n * @param tileTop 本格 tileTop（DRAW_Y_OFFSET 恒 2）\n */\nexport function drawCageCell(\n  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: { ensureVImage: (f: string) => HTMLImageElement | null },\n  fam: CageFamily, r: DrawSource, x: number, y: number,\n  px: number, py: number, tileTop: number, sheetH: number,\n): void {\n  const rows = Math.max(1, Math.floor(sheetH / fam.step));\n  const slot = cageSlot(fam.slot, x, y, r.sx, r.sy - Math.floor(r.sy / fam.step) * fam.step);\n  // 注意：r.sy 此时尚未含动画帧行偏移——槽位哈希要用物体的存储帧（本格在物体内的\n  // 行偏移），故先剥掉可能的帧行再算。此处传入的本格源 Y 即存储帧 Y（调用方未加\n  // 动画偏移），直接取用即可\n  const row = cageFrameRow(fam.idx, slot, tileAnim.tick, rows);\n  const addFrY = row * fam.step;\n  // 710 河豚笼 addFrX 横带（TileDrawing.cs:5840-5843）：帧 ≥33 才换列，本表 33 带\n  // 恰好单列，恒 0\n  const asx = r.sx;\n  const asy = r.sy + addFrY;\n  if (asy + r.sh > r.img.height) return;  // 帧行越界（表残缺）：整格跳过防画出邻带\n  const baseY = py + tileTop;\n  const isTopRow = asy % fam.step === 0;  // 当前帧行首行格（:1673/:1675 的 %54/%36）\n  if (!isTopRow || fam.lid < 0) {\n    ctx.drawImage(r.img, asx, asy, r.sw, r.sh, px, baseY, r.sw, r.sh);\n    return;\n  }\n  // 首行格：本体下移 8px（源裁顶 8px，:1679-1686）+ 顶盖画在 tileTop-2（:1687-1694）\n  ctx.drawImage(r.img, asx, asy + 8, r.sw, r.sh - 8, px, baseY + 8, r.sw, r.sh - 8);\n  const lid = atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/CageTop_${fam.lid}.png`);\n  if (!lid) return;\n  // style 0 顶盖 X 取源 X %108（剥河豚横带；:1690-1693）；宽 16 高 10（:1694-1696）\n  const lidX = fam.lid === 0 ? asx % 108 : asx;\n  if (lidX + 16 > lid.width) return;\n  ctx.drawImage(lid, lidX, 0, 16, 10, px, baseY - 2, 16, 10);\n}\n"
}

// 仙人掌帧回归:原版 WorldGen.CactusFrame(1456 :56354-56560)1:1 的确定性验证
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';
import { cactusFrameAt } from '../src/render/VanillaTiler';
import { TILE_BY_KEY } from '../src/data/tiles';

const CACT = TILE_BY_KEY['v_80_cactus']!;
const DIRT = TILE_BY_KEY['dirt']!;

function store(): TileStore {
  return new TileStore(40, 40);
}

describe('仙人掌帧(原版 CactusFrame)', () => {
  it('纯主干:顶格 (0,0) / 中格 (0,18) / 底格(下方实心) (0,36)', () => {
    const st = store();
    // 3 高主干在 (20, 30..32),下方沙/泥
    for (let y = 30; y <= 32; y++) st.setTileSilent(20, y, CACT);
    st.setTileSilent(20, 33, DIRT);
    expect(cactusFrameAt(st, 20, 30, CACT)).toEqual({ col: 0, row: 0 });   // 顶
    expect(cactusFrameAt(st, 20, 31, CACT)).toEqual({ col: 0, row: 1 });   // 中(fy=18)
    expect(cactusFrameAt(st, 20, 32, CACT)).toEqual({ col: 0, row: 2 });   // 底(fy=36)
  });

  it('主干顶带西臂(下方无对角臂根):顶格 (72,0);身格臂不在本行 → (0,18)', () => {
    const st = store();
    for (let y = 30; y <= 32; y++) st.setTileSilent(20, y, CACT);
    st.setTileSilent(19, 30, CACT);   // 西臂仅与主干顶同行
    st.setTileSilent(20, 33, DIRT);
    expect(cactusFrameAt(st, 20, 30, CACT)).toEqual({ col: 4, row: 0 });   // fx=72,fy=0

```

'_Party' : ''}.png`;\n  if (alt === 2 && p.transform) return `${root}_Default_Transformed.png`;\n  return `${root}_Default.png`;\n}",
 "new_string": "export function townNpcProfileSheet(id: number, variant: number, alt: number): string | null {\n  const p = TOWN_NPC_PROFILE[id];\n  if (!p) return null;\n  // 微光档仅对有 StackedNPCProfile 的 29 只生效；裸 LegacyNPCProfile（slime 族）\n  // 不看 townNpcVariationIndex，恒走常态档\n  const useShimmer = variant === 1 && p.shimmer;\n  const root = useShimmer ? `vanilla/Shimmered_${p.name}` : `vanilla/${p.name}`;\n  const uniqueParty = useShimmer ? p.shimmerParty : p.party;\n  if (alt === 1) return `${root}_Default${uniqueParty ? '_Party' : ''}.png`;\n  if (alt === 2 && p.transform) return `${root}_Default_Transformed.png`;\n  return `${root}_Default.png`;\n}"
}

181	export function cageFrameRow(family: number, slot: number, tick: number, frames: number): number {
182	  if (frames <= 1) return 0;
183	  const episode = Math.floor(tick / 65536);                       // 换拍周期
184	  const idle = 30 + hash32(family * 131 + slot, episode) % 871;   // rand.Next(30, 900)
185	  const action = (frames - 1) * ACTION_TICKS;
186	  const cycle = idle + action;
187	  const t = ((tick % cycle) + cycle) % cycle;
188	  if (t < idle) return 0;
189	  return 1 + Math.min(frames - 2, Math.floor((t - idle) / ACTION_TICKS));
190	}

213	  const row = cageFrameRow(fam.idx, slot, tileAnim.tick, rows);
214	  const asx = r.sx;
215	  const asy = r.sy + row * fam.step;
216	  if (asy + r.sh > r.img.height) return;  // 帧行越界（表残缺）：整格跳过防画出邻带
217	  const baseY = py + tileTop;
218	  const isTopRow = asy % fam.step === 0;  // 当前帧行首行格（:1673/:1675 的 %54/%36）
219	  if (!isTopRow || fam.lid < 0) {
220	    ctx.drawImage(r.img, asx, asy, r.sw, r.sh, px, baseY, r.sw, r.sh);
221	    return;
222	  }
223	  // 首行格：本体下移 8px（源裁顶 8px，:1679-1686）+ 顶盖画在 tileTop-2（:1687-1694）
224	  ctx.drawImage(r.img, asx, asy + 8, r.sw, r.sh - 8, px, baseY + 8, r.sw, r.sh - 8);
225	  const lid = atlas.ensureVImage(`vanilla/CageTop_${fam.lid}.png`);
226	  if (!lid) return;
227	  // style 0 顶盖 X 取源 X %108（剥河豚横带；:1690-1693）；宽 16 高 10（:1694-1696）
228	  const lidX = fam.lid === 0 ? asx % 108 : asx;
229	  if (lidX + 16 > lid.width) return;
230	  ctx.drawImage(lid, lidX, 0, 16, 10, px, baseY - 2, 16, 10);
231	}
232
